# Skills · E · part 97

768 entries, explain-it to explainer-isotype-narrative. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **explain-it**: Turn research, a plan, options, a comparison, results or a progress report into one diagram-first, plain-English HTML page the reader can understand fast and decide on. Use when the user says "explain it", "help me understand", "make this easy to digest", "show me the plan", "put this on a page", or when they must review or approve something before work continues. Do NOT use for short answers, small fixes or one-line status updates. ([NavSha/explain-it](https://github.com/NavSha/explain-it/tree/HEAD/skills/explain-it))
- **explain-it**: Use when user asks to be taught a technical concept ("teach me", "explain", "tutorial", "I don't understand", "rephrase simpler"), when user signals frustration with a prior explanation ("too long", "too much jargon", "wall of text"), or when user invokes /explain-it or /explain-it-now. ([Siriko404/explain-it](https://github.com/Siriko404/explain-it/tree/HEAD/skills/explain-it))
- **explain-it-back**: Make sure the user understands what was just built, by having them explain it and filling only the gaps. Use when the user says teach me, quiz me, explain this, I don't get it, walk me through it, or at the end of a feature they want to own. ([calebnewtonusc/ai-playbook](https://github.com/calebnewtonusc/ai-playbook/tree/HEAD/kit/skills/explain-it-back) · 3 copies)
- **explain-it-simply**: Turn any complex topic or question into a short, plain-language explainer video: a hook, an analogy, a few essential parts, and a payoff, carried almost entirely by kinetic typography and keyframed shapes since there's usually no product or screen recording to lean on. Use when the user says "explain X simply", "make a video explaining how X works", "turn this concept into a video", or asks for a short explainer on something with no existing footage or article behind it. ([clueso-ai/skills](https://github.com/clueso-ai/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/explain-it-simply) · ★31 · 2 copies)
- **explain-it-to-me**: Explains technical concepts introduced while building software and records concise, linked Markdown notes in a personal Obsidian vault.
